### Wiki
With a high fire rate, ammo efficiency and rapid reload when emptied, this unusual sidearm can hand out carnage all day long. Ammo efficiency is further increased when Protea uses this, her signature weapon.
Velox is Protea's signature automatic sidearm with high fire rate, innate 20% Ammo Efficiency, and faster reload from an empty magazine. In the hands of Protea, Velox's Ammo Efficiency is increased to 40%.
This weapon can be sold for 5,000.

Velox Prime
The Velox's component parts can be acquired from within the Granum Void; the Barrel from Rotation C, the Receiver from Rotation C of Extended Granum Void. The main blueprint can be purchased from the Market. All components, except for the blueprint, can be traded between players.
**Notes:**
Ammo efficiency is based on number of shots fired, and the free shot interval is carried over between magazine reloads.
Although its noise level is listed as "alarming," the weapon is actually silent and behaves like other silent weapons. However, it will still break Ivara's Prowl unless the Suppress mod is added. (Bug?)

## Vesper 77
A true masterpiece of Höllvanian engineering, Vesper 77's advanced laser sight highlights enemy Weak Points with precision. Aim to increase its Critical Damage Multiplier.

### Wiki
Tenno
A true masterpiece of Höllvanian engineering. Vesper 77's advanced laser sight highlights enemy Weak Points with precision. Aim to increase its Critical Damage Multiplier.
The Vesper 77 is a 1999 semi-automatic pistol with extreme accuracy and an included suppressor. Aiming activates the laser sight to highlight weak points with increased critical multiplier.
Characteristics
This weapon deals primarily Puncture damage.
Aiming highlights weak points indicated by crosshairs and grants +40% Critical Damage.
The color of the targeting reticle that appears on enemy head/weakspots is dictated by the energy color of the weapon.
Can use the Vesper 77-exclusive Leaded Gas mod.
Silent.
Vesper 77's main blueprint is awarded from Höllvania Central Mall Bounties as a rare drop, requiring the completion of The Hex to access. The Barrel, Receiver, and Handle are awarded from Höllvania Legacyte Harvest and Hell-Scrub.
Alternatively Vesper 77's blueprints can be purchased from Amir of The Hex for Standing Standing; component blueprints for 5,000 Standing and the main blueprint for 15,000 Standing, all requiring Rank 3 - 2-For-1.
**Trivia:**
The Vesper 77 is based on the Polish PT83, derived from the P-83 Wanad, or the Russian PB, that has a fixed suppressor for the barrel and is designed so that the slide will remain closed when firing, necessitating manual racking of the slide to eject the casing. Key deviations from the real-world design include a fully-reciprocating slide, an underbarrel mounting point for the laser sight, more modern materials, and a general increase in size so as to fit at the scale of Warframes.
The Höllvanian text on the right reads "OoRNIhNg MAeH FAyeHEhR OoHEhN UhNLOhDOhD" (Warning may fire when unloaded) and ".45 KAhL" (.45 Cal). The text on the left side reads "PAhTUhNTEhD SehP 12 1966, NOhV 4 1968, PR 15 1976" (Patented Sep 12 1966, Nov 4 1968, Apr 15 1976), "EhN 17 0451" (N 17 0451), and "VEhSPEhR MAwDUhL 77" (Vesper Model 77).
**Type:** Pistol | **Mastery Req:** 14 | **Riven Disposition:** 2

## Viper
A full-auto pistol known as the most compact and lightweight weapon of the Grineer arsenal. Though it has a small Magazine Capacity, the Viper has a very high Fire Rate and ultra-fast reload times thanks to a simplified magazine design.

### Acquisition
The Viper's blueprint can be purchased from the Market.

### Wiki
Weapons
A full-auto pistol known as the most compact and lightweight weapon of the Grineer arsenal. Though it has a small Magazine Capacity, the Viper has a very high Fire Rate and ultra-fast reload times thanks to a simplified magazine design.
The Viper is a machine pistol of Grineer design issued to their Shield Lancers. It is the most portable weapon manufactured by the Grineer and boasts a high rate of fire and an extremely efficient magazine reload, but poor accuracy.
This weapon can be sold for 2,500; it is also a weapon crafting ingredient for Twin Vipers (x2) and Hystrix.
Bolto + Viper → Hystrix
Viper + Viper → Twin Vipers
Characteristics
This weapon deals primarily Impact damage.
Can use the Viper-exclusive Skull Shots and Stinging Truth mods.
Innate Madurai polarity.
Tied with Viper Wraith for fastest fire rate of all single-handed pistols.
Mediocre accuracy.
Inefficient ammo economy.
Twin Vipers
Viper Wraith
**Tips:**
Although it is not recommended for long range fighting, correctly aiming and tapping the trigger will expend 1-2 bullets at once, and you may get a decent critical or headshot rate by doing this.
This may be done more comfortably by making a secondary keybind for firing on mouse wheel. It shall also allow to fire in a way Shield Lancers do.
Using the Pistol Ammo Mutation mod can help regain lost ammo when needed. Alternatively, use Pistol Scavenger aura or bring along Squad Ammo Restore.
**Type:** Pistol | **Mastery Req:** 4 | **Riven Disposition:** 5

## Zakti
Fires razor-sharp darts that anchor themselves in their target before erupting in a plume of toxic gas.

### Acquisition
The Zakti's blueprint can be researched from the Tenno Lab in the dojo.

### Wiki
Weapons
Fires razor-sharp darts that anchor themselves in their target before erupting in a plume of toxic gas.
The Zakti Tenno pistol fires darts that explode with Gas damage after a short period, with a chance to proc. Each dart also has a guaranteed Impact proc, as well as the explosion causing enemies to stagger.
It can be sold for ‍5,000.
Characteristics
This weapon deals primarily Gas damage.
Darts have a guaranteed Impact proc on direct hits.
Darts explode in a 3.5 meter area radius after 1 second on impacting a surface or enemy, dealing Gas damage.
Innate Gas damage – effective against Infested Deimos and Techrot.
Exploding darts will stagger enemies, making them vulnerable to melee finishers and susceptible to stealth damage multipliers.
Initial hit and explosion apply status separately.
Explosion does not need direct line of sight to deal damage and will penetrate walls.
No Damage Falloff.
Can benefit from Fulmination (Primed Fulmination).
Two innate Naramon polarities.
High ammo efficiency.
Very low magazine capacity of just 3 rounds; requires frequent reloading.
High recoil, even more so when firing from the hip.
Darts have travel time.
Explosion delay allows enemies to move out of explosion range if not directly hit by one of the bolts.
Explosion inflicts self-stagger.
Explosion has a headshot multiplier of 1x and cannot trigger headshot conditions.
Zakti Prime

## Zymos
Infect your foes with this spore-scattering pistol. Headshots burrow in and explode, releasing spore clouds that seek out nearby enemies.

### Wiki
Weapons
Infect your foes with this spore-scattering pistol. Headshots burrow in and explode, releasing spore clouds that seek out nearby enemies.
Zymos is an Infested pistol that releases explosive spores. On headshots, the spores burrow into the target, stunning and dealing continuous damage before exploding to release 6 additional spores that seek nearby enemies.
Characteristics
This weapon deals primarily Puncture damage.
Advantages:
High Puncture and good Impact damage – effective against armor and shields.
Above average critical multiplier.
Very high status chance.
Shots have a guaranteed Impact proc.
Shots explode in a 3.3 meter radius upon impact with a surface or enemy, inflicting Toxin damage.
Innate Toxin damage – bypasses Shields.
Headshots stun targets for 1.1 second and deals continuous damage before exploding for 953 Toxin damage and knocking down the initial target, then scattering 6 spores that home-in on nearby enemies within 20 meters, dealing 23 damage on contact with a guaranteed chance to proc Impact on targets over 7m away from the initial target in addition to 333 Toxin damage in a radius each.
Initial hit and explosion apply status separately.
Explosion does not need direct line of sight to deal damage and will penetrate walls.
Can benefit from Fulmination (Primed Fulmination).
Innate Madurai and Naramon polarities.
Disadvantages:
Very low critical chance.
Very low fire rate.
Very slow reload speed.
All projectiles have travel time.
Explosions inflict self-stagger.
Explosion has linear damage falloff from 100% to 70% from central impact.
Headshot explosion delay allows enemies to move out of the explosion range.
Extremely low ammo capacity; requires just 3 reloads to fully deplete all ammo reserves.
